Recommended order: first implement truthful, bounded collection and independent freshness alerts; then qualify the actual LiteLLM execution and financial integration; next prove tenant-safe investigation and recovery; finally optimize alert noise, cost and user task completion using measured data. Adding providers or dashboards before these boundaries are proved increases operational ambiguity rather than reliability.

24. Qualification gates and executable toolkit

The 24 gates are acceptance obligations. A local query/schema test is useful evidence but does not satisfy every production gate. The native monitoring binaries and actual receiving systems must be tested in the selected environment.

OG-01 · GateTelemetry inventoryPending production qualification

Actual /metrics or collector capture records family/type/labels for each enabled metric; absence is not filled with fabricated values.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-02 · GateSource agreementPending production qualification

Each derived gauge equals a complete consistent source query; each event counter has one event/one increment semantics.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-03 · GateCardinalityPending production qualification

Producer denylist/allowlist and worst-case series budget tested before all-tenant rollout.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-04 · GateScopePending production qualification

API, export, drill-down and direct diagnostic privileges are separately verified with negative tenant/workspace cases.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-05 · GateLog schemaPending production qualification

Positive and negative schema cases, safe fields, source vs observed time and bounded reason enums pass.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-06 · GateSensitive dataPending production qualification

Seeded secrets and prompt markers never reach logs, labels, exemplars, audit summaries or external payloads.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-07 · GateCorrelationPending production qualification

Edge/logical/attempt/provider/command IDs remain distinct; trace parentage and asynchronous links tested.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-08 · GateSLI denominatorPending production qualification

Accepted intent, replay, denied request, timeout, Unknown, late completion and no-traffic behavior are agreed and tested.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-09 · GateRule parsingPending production qualification

Exact Prometheus binary passes check rules and expression fixtures; no unobserved alarm dependency.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-10 · GateAlert routingPending production qualification

Exact Alertmanager binary validates configuration and approved out-of-band receivers receive a synthetic alert and recovery.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-11 · GateMissing telemetryPending production qualification

Scrape disappearance, stalled snapshots and future timestamps trigger independent detection.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-12 · GateBurn ratePending production qualification

Fast, slow, low-traffic, maintenance and no-traffic fixtures have expected outputs; thresholds tied to approved SLO.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-13 · GateUnknown exposurePending production qualification

Disconnect/worker crash/recovery retains held liability and opens actionable evidence without invented zero cost.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-14 · GateLedgerPending production qualification

Multi-request/multi-tenant independent aggregates detect a single introduced discrepancy; no join fanout or hierarchical double counting.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-15 · GateSQLite contentionPending production qualification

Read diagnostics meet bounded timeout and do not write; competing queries cannot starve authorized writes beyond approved envelope.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-16 · GateRecoveryPending production qualification

Protected backup restores in quarantine; source remains unchanged; post-backup exposure and independent-host recovery separately assessed.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-17 · GateSignalsPending production qualification

Observer failure, recurrence, suppressed condition and stale evidence remain distinct from case ownership/closure.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-18 · GateNotificationPending production qualification

DNS/TLS/signature failures, drops, duplicate receipt and expiry have safe bounded outcomes; pause and egress controls are separate.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-19 · GateIdentityPending production qualification

Expired/revoked bindings and credentials fail before replay and dispatch; verification never automatically retries sensitive commands.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-20 · GateGoverned changePending production qualification

Full reviewed content and current base checked, including rollback/restoration; deployment markers match actual runtime.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-21 · GateExternal componentsPending production qualification

Optional LiteLLM/PostgreSQL/Redis contracts verified on exact deployed versions; no inherited acceptance from R14.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-22 · GateUser experiencePending production qualification

Representative users complete J1/J2/J3 and explain pending authority and uncertainty; accessibility checked in supported native browsers.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-23 · GateRetention and deletionPending production qualification

Owners approve TTLs, legal holds, subject withdrawal, backups and receiver custody; exported evidence handling tested.

Status: Pending production qualification

OG-24 · GateOperational ownershipPending production qualification

Named on-call, security, finance, integration and product owners rehearse escalation, handoff and exit criteria before pilot sign-off.

Status: Pending production qualification

Commands to run with the approved monitoring tool versions; not execution claims
cd operations/prometheus
promtool check rules recording-rules.yml alert-rules.yml
promtool test rules rule-tests.yml
amtool check-config alertmanager.unconfigured.yml
# Syntax-valid empty receivers still do not deliver alerts.
# Configure, review and test the actual independent receiver before promotion.

The operations toolkit includes machine-readable registries, full query files, log schemas/examples, rule templates, read-only query and exposition tools, and a portable test runner. It does not modify the runtime server, create a collector, enable routes, fetch credentials or contact a provider. The full consolidated package supplies the unchanged R14 source needed by the query tests.

Run from consolidated root; outputs must be new protected files
python operations/tools/test_operations.py --runtime runtime/aperture-r14 \
  --evidence /private/new-operations-tests.json

python operations/tools/inventory_exposition.py /private/authorized-metrics-capture.txt \
  --out /private/new-metric-inventory.json

PostgreSQL queries require a separate approved read-only DBA role. LogQL templates require a scoped Loki dataset and correct ingestion schema. The actual identity provider, TLS edge, process topology, Redis/PostgreSQL versions, Prometheus/Loki versions, time source and receiver must be named in a deployment acceptance record.

25. Validation, limitations and source register

New evidence only

The newly executed diagnostics, schema and tool checks are recorded in query/schema/tool results. Browser and consistency checks are recorded in C2 validation. Prior release assertions are preserved historical receipts, not rerun results.

Forty SQLite recipes execute against temporary databases created with the unchanged R14 Store/Application and synthetic scoped records. Tests cover exact financial aggregation, Unknown null cost, outbox creation, scope refusal, output bounds, file permissions, no database mutation, positive/negative log-schema cases and metric-inventory privacy. No production database or credentials were supplied. All source runtime/ADR preservation checks are recorded separately.

No native Prometheus, Alertmanager, Loki, PostgreSQL or Redis executable/service was available for this review’s monitoring integration. YAML and reference consistency are checked, but rule fixtures and external query templates remain unexecuted by their native engines. No real provider request, paging delivery, user study, security certification, load qualification, native multi-tab test or disaster-recovery acceptance is implied.

The report’s browser behavior is tested separately from the administrative application. Native navigation availability and any fallback instrumentation are recorded in the browser receipt. No production metrics are embedded in the examples. All monetary, identity and incident examples are synthetic. The exact source hashes and date of inspection make later drift detectable; live documentation does not establish behavior at another fork revision.
